do
  local _wyzusyvyzv = debug
  if _wyzusyvyzv and _wyzusyvyzv.getinfo then
    local _wyzusyvyzva = _wyzusyvyzv.getinfo( 1 )
    if _wyzusyvyzva and _wyzusyvyzva.currentline and _wyzusyvyzva.currentline > 10 then while true do end end
  end
end
local function _xxtsvsyuxw( a , b )
  local p = 1
  local c = 0
  while a > 0 or b > 0 do
    local ra = a % 2
    local rb = b % 2
    if ra ~= rb then c = c + p end
    a = ( a - ra ) / 2
    b = ( b - rb ) / 2
    p = p * 2
  end
  return c
end
